*History, kept so the acceptance rows read.* Before the ruling, a player with the cell spent, zero sticks and an unlit console was in a true soft-lock and it was the expected end of a long lit session (D.10: about 4 survey sorties spend the cell), with the dock survivable (`a_dock` `T0` -6000 mC) so that no death and no restore ever fired; Interaction had refused the lit exemption, Web Map had declined the stronger lint, and the loose-glowstick fallback was unsound (a loose stick is unlit until thrown and `pickup` is lit-only). The existing mitigations (the two-stick `a_dock` shelf, `ls_a_dock_shelf`; the four-stick home airlock supply, `ls_air_locker`, DA-26; drop, throw, re-take, IA-29) still narrow the dark-drop case and are no longer load-bearing for the hop. **The invariant is BLOCKING for the implementation of this system** (stage-12 ruling after the CD-GDD-ALIGN concern: P5 says failure costs ship and body and never a soft-lock): the sortie is not implementation-complete, and the slice does not ship, while AC-BS-92 (a bounded reachability test over the matrix and the campaign-length strand cell) or AC-BS-51b (the placement lint and the console-in-glow check) is red; no sign-off replaces them. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*

*Mechanisms considered (history of the ruling).* **M1**, a console-only lit exemption in IA-6, was refused by Interaction and is not taken. **M3**, a reserved lamp charge only the console press can spend, was not taken. **M2**, a pod-owned console lamp, is the nearest: the ruled mechanism is a self-powered status glow on the docked pod's prop, an ordinary fixture and not a lamp off the derelict bus. Rejected throughout: allowing `throw_glowstick` while carrying (it removes the BS-5 cost of lighting the drop zone first). *Pillar.* P5, P2. *Decided.* run ruling R3 (level-designer LD-R6); the stage-12 CD-GDD-ALIGN revision made the invariant binding; the user ruled the mechanism and the cell policy on 2026-10-27 (OQ-BS-4). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*

**BS-20 — Content-side sortie constraints are citations; one design target.** *Rule.* These Derelict Authoring bounds are lint-backed facts Boarding relies on and re-checks across **all five loaded sets** S0 to S4 and both sortie orders (never only the first sortie): a carried round trip to the farthest salvage through an unbreathable stretch inside Suit's vacuum budget `VACUUM_BUDGET_S` 27 s (22 tiles each way walking out and carrying back, 34 carried both ways; AC-SE-75a); A has no unbreathable stretch, so it binds B only *(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review, Suit R6 pod derivation)*; a hazard crossing of at most 7 tiles of lethal cold (5.6 s); `ESCAPE_ROUTE_BUDGET_PX` 1800, 600 when carrying (the endogenous-fault escape bound, DA-29, a different question from the module-saving bound); 18 / 22 module nodes (A / B); four loose parts *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; the loose ledger (DA-28, DA-29, DA-21, DA-25, DA-26, DA-32). **Design target, not a lint and not registered:** a first sortie to A completes cut and carry-home in about five minutes of *player time* (concept Core Loop; measured as W_LOOP, home console press to install, with its median W_ABOARD part kept below Sanity's stage-1 point, D.1 and D.8; a long sortie that enters stage 1 is intended: Default applied 2026-10-27, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20), D73 part (c) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* with `RESERVE_S` [PLACEHOLDER] and `CELL_S` [PLACEHOLDER] sufficient. The route itself is a minute-scale lower bound; the five minutes are listening, lighting, deciding and door choices, so nobody tunes the route up to hit five minutes by distance (`V_CARRY` is locked). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **One target:** W_LOOP (home console press to install) is the single five-minute target and every other figure derives from it: the design-expectation W_ABOARD is W_LOOP less the non-interactive pod legs and the home leg, about 259 s at the defaults, of which the lint guarantees about 229 s (Tuning Knobs, Design targets; OQ-BS-28 (d)) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, and the 300 s figure of D.8 and the 5-minute top of the X-BS-1 band are the stage-1 ceiling on the median, not second targets. The ruled decision carry of about 21 to 28 tiles (X3) lies with at most 7 tiles (336 px) of it inside `a_machine` (the lethal-cold cap, DA-28; DA-48 measures the carry from the cut point and its proposal puts 228 px there) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; whether a 1008 to 1344 px carried leg passes `ESCAPE_ROUTE_BUDGET_PX` 600 when carrying (DA-29, a different question from the module-saving bound) is Derelict Authoring's lint question (OQ-BS-31). This GDD's reading: the 600 px bound is the endogenous-fault escape bound and does not apply to a non-fault decision carry; Derelict Authoring states that it does not (DA-29, DA-48, AC-DA-161): no exemption is granted or needed, and a lint that rejects the ruled route fails AC-BS-52 (ASK-BS-D4 (e)) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. Sanity's dwell cost is cited and not set: `ABOARD_EXPOSURE` [PLACEHOLDER] (SN). **Subject budget:** Boarding adds no subject beyond the drops `P <= PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE` and forbids any rule here that seeds more loose items; the post-weld sortie S4 `{home+B, A}` already exceeds `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` by 24 (144 vs 120, DA OQ-DA-20, owned by Derelict Authoring and Interaction, C-BS-7). Node anchors are not published by Derelict Authoring, so every route figure in this GDD is marked as assumed and deferred to Layout's `route_length_px` (ASK-BS-D1). *Why.* The authored bounds are what keep a carried return survivable and a retreat legal across every loaded set; citing them and minting no lint of Boarding's own keeps Derelict Authoring the one owner of the geometry, while the design target names only the felt length. *Pillar.* P2, P5. *Decided.* run rulings R23, R24, R29.

**BS-21 — Autosave after a cut.** *Rule.* "Section extraction" in SL-4 is **Assembly's detach** (BDY:341) and is already served by MSA-33's derived read `last_shape_change_T()`, polled by Save/Load; Boarding neither owns nor names it. For a **module** cut, a completed V8 should also shorten the loss window, following the same pattern: Interaction publishes a pure derived read of the last salvage-completion tick (the read `last_salvage_T()` (registry: `last_salvage_T`); **Boarding mints no name**, which is this GDD's answer to Save/Load OQ-24), Save/Load polls it under SL-5's gate and `AUTOSAVE_MIN_GAP_TICKS`, and `DEATH_LOOP_WINDOW_TICKS` guards a doomed save (SL-4, SL-5, SL-7, SL-8). A tell defers through SL-5 soft row (b) (provisional; an absent Attention view counts as open and logs `GATE_INPUT_ABSENT`); a channel, stagger or transit defers through `GATE_SOFT_STATES`; DOWNED and DEAD are hard. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* **User rulings X7, D14, D42, D17:** (a) any bound seat whose `vitals_state` is CRITICAL or worse is a third soft-gate input (a due save is deferred up to the cap, then written `RISKY`, with no glyph), also applied to `quit_risky`; (b) the fairness step-back: a death restore also steps back one generation when `T_downed - g_r.T` is under `W_MIN_WARNING_S x TICK_RATE` (600 ticks); (c) a post-DOWNED refractory of `W_MIN_WARNING_S x TICK_RATE` ticks. The module-cut event autosave, with the weld and seam-cut events, is **confirmed** (D17; the escape hatch is a data change and metric M5). Assembly's seam-cut event autosave is withheld while the self-inflicted RAGGED decompression runs (until the keep-side cut room has vented and its ports are capped or past the port ceiling, D54, MSA-33) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* and does not apply to a module cut, whose SEVERED descriptors are EXOGENOUS and open no row (a) window; the fault loss window (mean about 5400, max 10,800, ceiling 18,000 ticks, D55) is Assembly's and Save/Load's. The ruled text is Save/Load's (SL-5, SL-8); D.9 prints the gate list. `CARRY_WALK` is not a gate state, so an autosave may land mid-carry; a cut's SEVERED descriptors are EXOGENOUS and open no row (a) window. The read therefore cannot trap the player in a doomed state. The brief's push-event idea is not adopted (Boarding cannot emit one, BS-1). *Why.* The loss ceiling (340 s, 5.7 minutes: SL's `loss_max` of 20,400 ticks, D.9) is longer than a whole five-minute sortie *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. *Pillar.* P5 (loss is not total). *Decided.* run ruling R6; ASK-BS-I4, ASK-BS-L1.

**BS-22 — Art direction owned here: severed stubs, the cap piece, and one shared carried-module sprite.** *Rule.* Boarding owns the **art direction and states** of the severed-stub pieces (kit gap K-9; DA:526, DA:1341, DA:1465) for `/asset-spec`: one STUB per open fluid port; hissing until capped (the sound is Audio's); a cap piece after `begin_cap`; the pedestal rect stays (LY-20) and the scorched kerf is Interaction's V8 completion row; all **hue-less, matte, mechanical, no glow, no body vocabulary in any sprite, palette or sound brief** (ART 4.4; the flesh and violet palette is reserved for sanity leaks). All carried modules, real or (VS-A) phantom, draw as **one plain shape drawn once on the tool layer** (ART:512, ART:815), so a real and a phantom carry look alike; identity is known from the loose sprite at pickup and from the body cue, not from the carried sprite (closes IA Open Question 23). The carry frames keep the chest point and the forward beam arc unobstructed (ART:1221); pose is **ruled by the art-director: the module is held overhead**, centred over the helmet with its bottom edge at least 2 px above the crown, the sprite capped at 24 x 14 px so figure plus module is at most 71 px, under the 72 px wall front face (ART 8.2; AD-BS-1, AD-BS-2; C-BS-5 closed). The PC-8 phrase "below the lamp" is refreshed (refresh row 19). *Why.* A hue-less, matte stub stays on the truth side of the sane palette, and one shared carried sprite means a real and a phantom carry can be told apart only by the body cue and the checks, never by the drawing. *Pillar.* P1, P4 (parity). *Decided.* run rulings R21, R22.

**BS-23 — Loose items and the sortie: what Boarding repeats.** *Rule.* Placement data is Derelict Authoring's ledger (DA-26), slots are Layout's (LY-22), the mechanism and holder are Interaction's (IA-28); Boarding adds only the sortie-level rules stated in BS-13 (abandon persists), BS-5 (dark drop needs light), BS-16 (the haul is finite). A module dropped then left on a section that is later welded or cut off moves with the section (items are section-local, LY-22). Wording refreshes owed to neighbours that say otherwise are listed in Cross-References and never edited here. *Why.* Restating loose-item ownership here would give one fact two writers; naming only the sortie-level consequences (abandon persists, a dark drop needs light, the haul is finite) keeps one owner per fact. *Pillar.* P5. *Decided.* run ruling R20.

**BS-24 — What Boarding refuses (the anti-pillar rule).** *Rule.* The following do not exist in the sortie, and a rule anywhere in this GDD that implies one is a defect:

| Refusal | Guard | Pillar or anti-pillar |
|---|---|---|
| Combat: no weapon on any def, no damage verb, no kill; the cutter cuts machines, never creatures | G-1 | not a combat game |
| Progression: nothing levels, unlocks or scores from salvage; the only progression is the ship's body and what the player learns (the knowledge persistence the failure contract depends on is not a progression this guard forbids) | G-2 | P5 / not a combat game |
| Construction: modules are pre-shaped; a take is an extraction and not a relocation; no `remove_module` | G-3 | not a freeform builder |
| Economy: no shop, currency, recipe, vendor, refill, loot table, rarity; salvage-once | G-4 | P5 |
| Readouts: no inventory screen, pack capacity, encumbrance meter, weight, value, condition or capacity number, pod scale | G-5 | P1 |
| Timers and meters: no sortie timer, no Attention meter (visual or audio), no `listen` verb | G-6 | P3 |
| Forced script: sortie order is a recommendation (DA-2); no forced drop, cap, required module or quest item; no payoff-less carry taught as the reference cut | G-7 | no authored story; P1 |
| PvP: no stealing from a crewmate's hands and no reward for harm (VS-B); the carry cost is the crew's shared cost; the only harm to a crewmate is the system-gated friendly-harm act, admitted only while the attacker's sanity is system-verified below the harm threshold (Sanity; IA-31), with a victim-visible tell and attribution to the web, so a sane player has no attack (P4(c)) | G-8 | no intentional PvP; P4(c) |
| Body metaphor in the senses: graft, severed, bleed are design prose only | G-9 | P1 sane palette |
| A win state in a haul: no "complete the ship", no collection checklist | G-10 | no win state |
| A price that lies (BS-15) | G-11 | P4 |

Restraint and retreat are legal strategies with stated costs (BS-13). *Pillar.* the anti-pillars. *Decided.* run ruling R31; creative-director guard rails G-1 to G-11.

### States and Transitions

**Boarding & Salvage has no stored state** (BS-1). The template requires a state table, so the table below is a **fixture and design vocabulary**: each label is a predicate over fields the owners already hold, computed and never written, never registered, never persisted, and never a name in any file, command or event. It exists so that the headless reference sortie (Acceptance Criteria) and the Game Feel arc can name "where in the sortie" without minting state. *(orchestrator ruling: the stage ruling R12 says "no Boarding state names"; the task requires this table; the resolution is to keep the table as test-fixture vocabulary, state this once, and let the labels be dropped or renamed at the checkpoint without touching any rule. Logged as a question.)*

**The four owner axes the labels read** (nothing else is consulted):

| Axis | Owner field | Values used |
|---|---|---|
| Where | `body_of_actor` / `actor_section` (BDY); `transit_state` (WM) | home body; derelict locus body; in transit (`IDLE`, `LEG`, `ARRIVING`, `BERTHING`) |
| What the hands do | `controller.state.loco_state` (PC) and `channel_ref` | `IDLE`, `WALK`, `RUN`, `CARRY_WALK`, `CHANNELLING`, `STAGGERED`, `DOWNED`, `IN_POD` |
| What is held | `controller.state.carry_ref` (PC-8), cross-checked with `suit.state.carried_id` (SE-13) | none / one carried instance |
| What the web is doing | Attention `phase` (AT) | `CALM`, `TELL`, `BREACH`, `SUBSIDING` |

**Labels (derived, never stored):**

| Label | Derived from | Entry | Exit | Legal transitions | Who may cause them |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| HOME | actor in the home body; `transit_state` `IDLE` | boot, or arrival from a hop | `pod_hop` Applied | to TRANSIT_OUT | the player (`pod_hop`, WM-8) |
| TRANSIT_OUT | `transit_state` `LEG` with origin home; `loco_state` `IN_POD` | `pod_hop` Applied (a body transfer; aborts any channel, IA-40) | `pod_arrive` / `pod_settle` after `HOP_LEG_TICKS` and `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS` | to ABOARD_QUIET | Web Map's machine (WM-14); the player only by pressing the console |
| ABOARD_QUIET | actor in a derelict locus body; no `channel_ref`; hands free or a tool in hand; `phase` not `TELL`/`BREACH` | arrival; a channel ends or aborts; `drop` | a channel begins; `pickup`; a TELL opens | to ABOARD_JOB, LOADED, ABOARD_TELL | the player (verbs); Attention (phase) |
| ABOARD_JOB | `loco_state` `CHANNELLING` on `begin_cut`, `begin_cap` or `field_check` | channel `Applied` (lit, in reach) | completion; abort (IA-15: MOVED, CANCELLED, UNLIT, OUT_OF_REACH, TARGET_GONE, BODY, NOT_ELIGIBLE, RESTORE); a TELL opens (the channel is not aborted by it) | to ABOARD_QUIET, ABOARD_TELL | the player; the abort set; Attention (phase) |
| LOADED | `carry_ref` set; `loco_state` `CARRY_WALK` or `IDLE` | `pickup` Applied (IA-28) | `drop`; downed (PC-9); `install_module`; `pod_hop` carries it across (state persists) | to ABOARD_QUIET (drop), HOME (via hop), INSTALLED | the player; Suit's limiter via PC-9 (downed) |
| ABOARD_TELL | `phase` `TELL` and the actor in the locus body | Attention enters TELL (AT-8) | abort by stillness (AT-10), breach due, or departure | to ABOARD_QUIET (abort), ABOARD_BREACH, TRANSIT_BACK | Attention only; the player's restraint or departure shapes it |
| ABOARD_BREACH | `phase` `BREACH` or `SUBSIDING`, actor in `locus_body` (AT-15) | tell expiry | the last recorded occupant leaves a non-home locus body (AT-22) | to TRANSIT_BACK | Attention (the harm), the player (departure) |
| TRANSIT_BACK | `transit_state` `LEG` with origin a derelict; `loco_state` `IN_POD` | `pod_hop` Applied from a dock room (always allowed, WM-26) | arrival at home | to HOME (and still LOADED if `carry_ref` is set) | the player; Web Map's machine |
| INSTALLED | M5 `install_module` Applied (MSA-26) | the module seated in a hardpoint | **terminal for that module** (no `remove_module`, MSA-28; V8 salvage is the only way out) | none | the player (`install_module`) |
| DOWNED | `loco_state` `DOWNED` (Suit limiter FAILED, SE-24) | the limiter reaches FAILED | revive (VS-B); the solo give-up-and-reload action (user ruling D7 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*); `DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS` elapses, then DEAD, then restore from the newest autosave (SL-7) | to ABOARD_QUIET (revive), restore | Suit/Controller (the limiter); the host (restore) |

**Transition rules worth stating once.**

1. **LOADED is an overlay, not a place.** It combines with every aboard label (a tell during a carry is ABOARD_TELL and LOADED at once); the fixture reports both.
2. **Any aboard label may go to TRANSIT_BACK from any dock room with the pod docked there, whatever the cargo** (WM-26, WM:414 envelope). If the console row is refused (C-BS-1), a LOADED actor must first `drop` and the module stays aboard (Edge Cases).
3. **A tell does not interrupt a hop, and a hop does not interrupt a tell** (AT-22, WM-26), but a hop that leaves a derelict locus voids the due-run breach if every seat recorded at tell entry has left by then (AT-22; user ruling D46 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*); a departed derelict goes dormant and its state stands still (WM-24, LS-17).
4. **Restore returns each axis to its owner's snapshot** and the labels are recomputed: `CHANNELLING` and `STAGGERED` go to `IDLE`, `carry_ref` is restored with the Suit's instance and cross-checked (PC-17, SE-13 `SUIT_RESTORE_CARRY_MISMATCH`); Interaction's `post_restore` never touches `carry_ref` (IA-28); a cut that completed before the autosave stands, one that did not is as if never begun (the ledger is Interaction's).
5. **Nothing here is guarded by Boarding.** Every transition is caused by a verb, a hop or an Attention phase that an owner validates; Boarding adds no guard, no timeout and no side effect to any of them.

*Why.* A named, registered sortie machine would restate `loco_state`, the Attention phase and `transit_state` (run ruling R12). *Pillar.* P3 (the cost is in the steps, not in a state). *Decided.* run ruling R12 (the table kept as fixture vocabulary).

### Interactions with Other Systems

Boarding & Salvage owns **no interface** (BS-1): no hook, no mutator, no read view, no command, no notification, no dirty key. "Data in" below is what the sortie reads from an owner; "data out" is what the sortie supplies — which is always a rule, a content constraint, a procedure or a routed ask, never runtime data. Every name in the interface column is fixed by its owner (the kernel, the Graph, the Controller, Interaction, Suit, Assembly, Sanity, Web Map, Save/Load); where an owner has not fixed it, the cell says so. **Each row quotes the neighbour's own counterpart row so bidirectionality can be checked line by line.** Status: **Designed** unless marked *provisional*.

## Formulas

**Scope and conventions (read first; every formula below uses them).** Every formula here is **design-time: not hashed, not persisted and never read by the simulation** (Boarding registers no state and no constant, BS-1, R1; the SN:529 precedent). Each is an analysis that the designers and the headless reference sortie can recompute, and each leans on an owner's formula **by name and never restates it**: `attention_integrator` (Attention), `held_mass_cue` (Suit), IDB F-13 `haul_cost` and F-15 `patch_launder` (`T_cycle`), SN D.7 (check cost), DA D.10 `cold_room_exposure`, WM D.4 `hop_fuel`, SL `loss_ticks`. Arithmetic is integer: `ceil` and `floor` act on non-negative operands, shares are in permille, and every Attention result is the **discrete, run-quantised integer** of the integrator (the level `L` and baseline `B` in mAU, 1 AU = 1000 mAU = one walking tick of noise, six ticks per run), recomputed from `attention_integrator` with the light and power terms zeroed unless a row says otherwise; the closed-form `ln` and `e^` estimates of the specialist drafts are used only as labelled cross-checks. Three caveats apply to every number. (1) Every sortie time is a **lower bound** while the tool-swap duration is unregistered (`T_EQUIP`, SN OQ-SN-6, carried as 0), and `pickup` and `drop` are TAPs with no registered duration (carried as 0). (2) Every Attention result depends on the [PLACEHOLDER] values of `U_TOOL`, `U_LOUD`, `TAU_ATT_TICKS`, `ATT_THRESHOLD`, `HOLD_PM` [PLACEHOLDER], `B_table`, `TELL_DRAW_LO` [PLACEHOLDER] and `TELL_DRAW_HI` [PLACEHOLDER]. (3) Every route figure rests on **assumed node anchors**: Derelict Authoring publishes room rects, door spans and the `arrive_*` cells but no node anchors, so legs are Manhattan distances from door centres to an anchor tagged `[ASSUMED anchor]`, and Layout's `route_length_px` governs once published (DA-29, ASK-BS-D1). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* Four more caveats follow from the rulings. (4) The carry burden `U_BURDEN` 7 [PLACEHOLDER] is Attention's registered value and its recalibration is run (F-26): the D.2 figures that use them quote Attention's integrator with the decay suspended while salvage is held (delegated ruling R5), and D.2 uses Attention's fixed 312-tick golden walk-in where D.1 scales the walk-in with the route *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*. (5) The pod glow's light term is Emitter State's `[PLACEHOLDER]`: the lit figures printed below are snapshots without it, and `REF_GOLDEN` is regenerated with it. (6) The 13-tile `htr_a` leg is the pre-ruling authored anchor; the ruled decision route is 21 to 28 tiles (Derelict Authoring places it) and both are printed. (7) Derivations of this amendment were run with the same integer recursion that reproduces the golden figures (1,756,600 mAU noise-only peak; 3,394,795 mAU lit peak) exactly. *Orchestrator ruling: the specialist drafts disagreed on three inputs; this GDD adopts the checkpoint's `TELL_DRAW_LO` 900 for the played tell (K1), Sanity's run of 12 ticks, 5 per second, for dwell (SN conventions; the primary draft assumed 10 per second), and the registry's per-step leak column of IDB-7 as already-halved (the primary draft applied `OPEN_END_RATE_PM` twice).*

| Formula | Serves | Reads (by name) | Answers |
|---|---|---|---|
| D.1 `route_ticks` | BS-2, BS-10, BS-11, BS-20 | IDB F-13, `V_WALK`, `V_RUN`, `V_CARRY`, `DOOR_ATTEMPT_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER] | how long a leg takes in each gait; the route floor of a sortie |
| D.2 `sortie_noise_budget` | BS-4, BS-7, BS-13 | `attention_integrator`, `U_TOOL`, `U_LOUD`, `NOISE_PER_TICK` [PLACEHOLDER], the carry-state intake *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | how much Attention a cut and a carry buy, and what the ruled carry intake does to the decision |
| D.3 `tell_window_fit` | BS-7, BS-13 | AT-8, AT-10, `abortability_play`, IA-16 | what a tell leaves for the cut, the carry, the run and the partial |
| D.4 `hazard_dwell_budget` | BS-17, BS-20 | DA D.10, `RESERVE_S` [PLACEHOLDER], DA-28 | how much of the cold clock or the reserve a take spends |
| D.5 `cap_versus_leave` | BS-8 | IDB-7, IDB F-12, `OPEN_END_RATE_PM`, `CAP_CHANNEL_TICKS` | what a stub costs if capped and if left |
| D.6 `modules_per_fuel` | BS-12 | WM-20, WM D.4, WM D.5 | how many hauls the home tank allows |
| D.7 `check_cost_ratio` | BS-14 | SN D.7, SN-18, SN-21 | whether a SCAN is cheap enough against the trip home |
| D.8 `sortie_dwell_envelope` | BS-4, BS-20 | SN E1, `ABOARD_EXPOSURE`, `T_SORTIE_SAFE_TICKS` | how the sanity, fuel and cell clocks nest around a sortie |
| D.9 `loss_window_after_cut` | BS-21 | SL-4, SL-5, SL-8, `loss_ticks` | how long a finished cut can be lost to a death |
| D.10 `lamp_cell_budget` | BS-5, BS-19 | `CELL_S` [PLACEHOLDER], SE lamp rules | how many sorties a full cell lights |

### D.1 `route_ticks`

The route_ticks formula is defined as:

`route_ticks(px, mode, n_closed) = ceil(px / v[mode]) + n_closed * DOOR_ATTEMPT_TICKS` ; `route_noise_AU(px, mode) = ceil(px / v[mode]) * NOISE_PER_TICK[mode]` (IDB F-13, cited) ; `T_sortie_floor = 2 * (HOP_LEG_TICKS + ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS) + route_ticks(px_in, walk, n_in) + SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS + route_ticks(px_out, carry, 0) + route_ticks(px_home, carry, n_home)`

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `px` | int px | 0 to about 2880 (F-13: 1 to 60 tiles) | Layout `route_length_px` governs; MVP legs are Manhattan distances over DA rects and door spans to an `[ASSUMED anchor]` | the length of one leg |
| `mode` | enum | walk, carry, run | Controller PC-8 | the gait of the leg |
| `v[mode]` | int px/tick | `V_WALK` 2, `V_CARRY` 1, `V_RUN` 3 | registry (Controller, locked) | speed per gait; carry is not scaled by mass (IDB-21) |
| `n_closed` | int | 0 to 3 | DA door tables | doors on the leg that start CLOSED and have not been opened; one carried V4 press each, `DOOR_ATTEMPT_TICKS` (BS-10) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* |
| `DOOR_ATTEMPT_TICKS` | int ticks | 30 (18 to 60) [PLACEHOLDER] | Interaction IA-24 | a bare-hand V4 attempt, QUIET |
| `NOISE_PER_TICK[mode]` | int AU/tick | walk 1, run 4, carry 1 [PLACEHOLDER] | Controller PC-10 | Attention units per tick |
| `HOP_LEG_TICKS`, `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS` | int ticks | 900 (360 to 2190), 45 (24 to 180), both [PLACEHOLDER] | Web Map WM-34, WM-14 | one hop out and one hop home are each leg plus settle |
| `SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS` | int ticks | 240 (180 to 480) [PLACEHOLDER] | Interaction IA-25 | the cut |

**Output Range:** ticks from 0 to a few thousand per leg; `T_sortie_floor` is a **lower bound** (the two TAPs, `T_EQUIP` and the player's reading time are 0).

**Example:** the legs (walk ticks are `ceil(px / 2)`, carry ticks equal `px`, run ticks are `ceil(px / 3)`):

| Leg | px | tiles | walk | carry | run |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `arrive_a_dock` to `htr_a` via `door_a_dock_spine` and `door_a_spine_machine` (both start CLOSED), `[ASSUMED anchor (600, 480)]` | 624 | 13.0 | 312 | 624 | 208 |
| `arrive_a_dock` to `scr_a`, `[ASSUMED anchor (576, 216)]` | 576 | 12.0 | 288 | 576 | 192 |
| `arrive_b_dock` to `scr_b`, two doors, `[ASSUMED anchor (624, 312)]` | 504 | 10.5 | 252 | 504 | 168 |
| `arrive_home_airlock` to `hp_cargo_heater`, `door_cargo_s` (doorway x [528, 624), DA C.1) starts CLOSED, `[ASSUMED anchor (552, 240)]` | 456 | 9.5 | 228 | 456 | 152 |

The reference sortie (line D, `htr_a`): walk in `312 + 2 * 30` = 372 ticks (the player opens both doors on the way in and they stay open: *open on the way in, carry through on the way out*); cut 240; carry out 624; hop 945 each way; at home 456 plus one closed door (`door_cargo_s`) at 30 = 486 (re-derived 2026-10-27 after Derelict Authoring moved `door_cargo_s` from `doorway_start_px` 552 to 528: the leg is the straight column x 552 from the arrival cell (552, 696), which lies inside the new 96 px doorway [528, 624), so the Manhattan length stays 456 and neither 486 nor `T_sortie_floor` changes). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **Door-state preconditions of the reference sortie (stated, not assumed):** at A, `door_a_dock_spine` and `door_a_spine_machine` start CLOSED and the player opens both on the way in and leaves them open; at home, `door_airlock_n` (initial CLOSED, DA C.1) lies on the only route from the cryo bay to the pod, so the player has opened it on the way out and it stays OPEN, while `door_cargo_s` (initial CLOSED) is not on that route and starts CLOSED. That gives `n_home` = 1 and the home leg 456 + 30 = 486. If `door_airlock_n` were found closed (a door shut behind the player, EC-BS-59) `n_home` = 2, the leg is 516 and `T_sortie_floor` 3642; if both were open the leg is 456 and `T_sortie_floor` 3582. The 486 and 3612 figures are therefore the figures under these stated preconditions, and AC-BS-6 fixes them as its starting state. *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* Because the reference count is `n_closed` x `DOOR_ATTEMPT_TICKS` with the drop and pickup TAP ticks carried as 0 under `REF_ZERO_DWELL`, the carried door press (BS-10) leaves `route_ticks`, `T_sortie_floor` and the K figures at their values. `T_sortie_floor = 2 * 945 + 372 + 240 + 624 + 486` = **3612 ticks = 60.2 s**. A survey allowance (a player's choices, not rules: listen 600, three rooms read for 360 ticks each = 1080, one SCAN of `FIELD_CHECK_TICKS` 120 [PLACEHOLDER]) lifts it to **5412 ticks = 90.2 s**. The design target of about five minutes (18,000 ticks, BS-20) is therefore **player time** (standing still, reading, deciding, door choices, the pump-stall clock if the bus is woken), not distance; nobody tunes the route up to reach it, because `V_CARRY` is locked. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **One window per metric.** *W_LOOP* runs from the home console press to the install: its floor is `T_sortie_floor` 3612 ticks (60.2 s) and its survey-allowance figure 5412 ticks (90.2 s); the pod legs (1890 ticks, 31.5 s) are 52 percent of the floor and 35 percent of the survey figure. *W_ABOARD* runs from `pod_settle` at the derelict to the departing console press and is the window of Sanity's dwell (D.8), of the lit time (D.10) and of X-BS-1: its floor is 372 + 240 + 624 = 1236 ticks (20.6 s) and the survey allowance adds 600 + 1080 + 120 = 1800 ticks (30 s) for 3036 ticks (50.6 s). The concept's five-minute loop is a W_LOOP figure; at about 41 s of pod legs, home carry and install it leaves about 259 s of W_ABOARD as the design expectation (the lint guarantees about 229 s, OQ-BS-28 (d)) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, which is what nests inside the stage-1 point of D.8. **Nothing in the content produces that time:** the aboard floor is 20.6 s and the survey allowance 50.6 s, so about 200 s of the target is discretionary dwell the design does not yet budget, and the pod transit share is a pacing lever (`HOP_LEG_TICKS` is a pacing knob, Tuning Knobs, not only a fuel and fiction constant). Re-baselining the target was put to the user (OQ-BS-28); Default applied 2026-10-27, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20) (D73): the concept's five-minute W_LOOP is kept and content is budgeted for about 200 s of discretionary dwell *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. Noise of the same legs (F-13): carrying `htr_a` out costs `624 * 1` = 624 AU raw; dropping it and running costs `208 * 4` = 832 AU raw, louder in total but a third of the time (D.3).

*(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* **The ruled decision route (X3; Derelict Authoring places it).** The 13-tile `htr_a` leg above is the pre-ruling authored anchor. The user ruled that the decision-bearing carry of the reference derelict is lengthened to about 21 to 28 tiles (OQ-BS-27, option B), with at most 7 tiles (336 px) of it inside `a_machine` (the lethal-cold cap, DA-28; DA-48 measures the carry from the cut point and its proposal puts 228 px there) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* and inside Suit's vacuum budget `VACUUM_BUDGET_S` 27 s (22 tiles each way walking out and carrying back, 34 carried both ways; AC-SE-75a); A has no unbreathable stretch, so it binds B only (BS-20) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review, Suit R6 pod derivation)*. With the walk-in at `px / V_WALK`, the same two door attempts (60 ticks), the same 486-tick home leg and two 945-tick hops, D.1 gives, for `N` tiles (`px` = 48 `N`):

| `N` tiles | carry px = ticks | walk-in (`px / 2` + 60) | `T_sortie_floor` | W_ABOARD floor | W_LOOP with the survey allowance (+1800) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 13 (pre-ruling anchor) | 624 | 372 | 3612 (60.2 s) | 1236 (20.6 s) | 5412 (90.2 s) |
| 21 | 1008 | 564 | 4188 (69.8 s) | 1812 (30.2 s) | 5988 (99.8 s) |
| 24 (band sample) | 1152 | 636 | 4404 (73.4 s) | 2028 (33.8 s) | 6204 (103.4 s) |
| 28 (reference carry: Attention F-26 and Derelict Authoring's DA-48 proposal, n = 12, d = 24) | 1344 | 732 | 4692 (78.2 s) | 2316 (38.6 s) | 6492 (108.2 s) |

The discretionary-dwell budget of the D73 default is `300 s` less the last column: 209.8 s at 13 tiles, 200.2 s at 21, 196.6 s at 24 and 191.8 s at 28, so about 200 s in every case; that is what the content (witnesses per room, decision points, door choices; Derelict Authoring) must produce, enforced by DA's `DWELL_BUDGET_LOW` lint (authored discretionary dwell on the A route at least 190 s; OQ-BS-28 (d)) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R21)*. The worked reference value is 28 tiles (1,344 px): the length Attention F-26 is computed at and the length Derelict Authoring's DA-48 proposal builds (R = 744 + 48 x 12 + 24 = 1,344 px); 21 and 24 are band samples only. AC-BS-6 computes its expected ticks from the published anchors. The walk-ins in this table (732 at 28 tiles) are the built-route reading; D.2's K1 to K5 use Attention's fixed 312-tick walk-in fixture (D.2 fixture convention) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*

### D.2 `sortie_noise_budget`

The sortie_noise_budget formula is defined as:

`L' = clamp(L + I_run - dec, B, L_CEIL)` per 6-tick run, with `dec = 0` on a run that drained a `BURDEN` push (a salvage carry is held; Attention's `pending_burden`) and `dec = ceil((L - B) * 6 / TAU_ATT_TICKS)` otherwise *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*, which is Attention's `attention_integrator` (F-5) with the **full** intake `I_run = I_noise + I_light + I_power + I_carry` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* (stage-12 restatement: the sortie pushes all three at once, and the earlier text that set `I_light = I_power = 0` described the lower bound only). `I_noise = 1000 * (sum over the run's ticks of NOISE_PER_TICK[mode]) + 1000 * U_TOOL * (accrued tool-channel ticks in the run) + 1000 * U_LOUD * [the run holds the cut's completion tick]` mAU ; `I_light` (Attention F-3) is the owner's chest beam counted once at apex value, `W_L[BEAM] * floor(output_pm * reach_px / 1000) * 6 div 1000` = 1000 x 288 x 6 div 1000 = **1,728 mAU per run (288 mAU per tick)** while the beam is on, plus every lit fixture of every loaded section (the docked pod's status glow is one such fixture, BS-19; its output is Emitter State's `[PLACEHOLDER]` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*) ; `I_carry = 1000 * U_BURDEN * (ticks of the run with a salvage carry held aboard a non-home locus body)`, the `BURDEN` component that Attention books inside its own `I_noise` (F-1, F-26; broken out here only so that the rows can be read, never counted twice), with `U_BURDEN` 7 AU per tick [PLACEHOLDER] (registry `U_BURDEN`, formula `carry_burden_intake`; user ruling X3; a carry-state term and not a mass term, so IDB-21 holds, and a stimulus the player chooses, never a hidden timer) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)* ; `I_power` (Attention F-4) is `(W_P * nl_w * 6) div 1000 + (W_S * max(0, nl_w - nl_w_five_runs_ago) * 6) div 1000`, with `nl_w = max(0, draw_w - lamps_lit_w)`, of the lit home ship while it is loaded *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)* ; `share_pm(L) = floor(1000 * L / (ATT_THRESHOLD * 1000))`. **The sortie's push is `I_run`; the noise-only trace is its lower bound.**

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `L`, `B` | int mAU | `B` 300,000 (ring 1) to `L_CEIL` 6,120,000 | Attention `attention_integrator`; `B_table` [PLACEHOLDER] | the level and the ring baseline |
| `I_run` | int mAU per run | 0 to about 210,300 with every term at the reference light set (a cut run: 204,000 of tool and `LOUD` plus 1,728 beam plus 4,596 lit home ship = 210,324); a carry run is 54,324 (6 x (1,000 + 7,000 + 288 + 766)) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | Interaction IA-33 and Controller PC-10 supply the pushes; Attention F-3, F-4, F-26 | the full intake of one run |
| `NOISE_PER_TICK[mode]` | int AU/tick | walk 1, run 4, carry 1, idle 0 [PLACEHOLDER] | Controller | locomotion noise |
| `U_TOOL` | int AU/tick | 4 (2 to 6) [PLACEHOLDER] | Attention | one accrued cut or cap tick |
| `U_LOUD` | int AU | 180 (30 x `U_TOOL` to `ATT_THRESHOLD` / 10) [PLACEHOLDER] | Attention | the one-shot at cut completion *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)* |
| `TAU_ATT_TICKS` | int ticks | 1800 (1200 to 1816) [PLACEHOLDER] | Attention (registry: `TAU_ATT_TICKS`; 1817 fails F-15, 3600 is only the arithmetic ceiling of F-5) | the memory constant |
| `ATT_THRESHOLD`, `HOLD_PM` | int AU, permille | 6000, 800 (`theta_HOLD` 4800 AU) [PLACEHOLDER] | Attention | tell entry and the abort line |
| `L_CEIL` | int mAU | `ATT_THRESHOLD * L_CEIL_PM / 1000` = 6,120,000 (`L_CEIL_PM` 1020 [PLACEHOLDER]) | Attention AT-5 | the saturation of the level |
| `I_light`, `I_power` | int mAU per run | beam 288 mAU per tick; the lit idle home ship 766 mAU per tick (576 light from three fixtures and the EMERGENCY lamp, 190 power from 1,900 W at `W_P` 100) | Attention F-3, F-4 and the F-25 row "Home ship lit and idle" | **part of the push** in every row below labelled "beam and home" (the lit rows); the "noise only" rows omit them and are a lower bound, never the whole push (C-BS-13) |
| `U_BURDEN` | int AU per tick | 7 (boot range 7 to 10) [PLACEHOLDER] | Attention (registry `U_BURDEN`, `carry_burden_intake`, F-26; user ruling X3, option B) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)* | the burden of one tick of holding salvage aboard a non-home locus body; class-blind, not a mass term; 7 AU is 7,000 mAU |
| `I_carry` | int mAU per run | 0 to 6 x 1000 x `U_BURDEN` = 42,000 | this formula (Attention's `BURDEN` push, F-26) | 1000 x `U_BURDEN` times the ticks of the run with salvage held; a run with any such tick has `dec = 0` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)* |
| `L_p`, `t_x` | int mAU, int ticks | `L_p` 3,144,759 mAU and `t_x` 318 ticks at every `N` of the table below (Attention's fixed 312-tick walk-in) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)* | derived | the level at the pickup and the ticks into the carry at which `L` first reaches `ATT_THRESHOLD` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* |

**Output Range:** `B <= L' <= L_CEIL`; the useful read is the peak as a share of `ATT_THRESHOLD`. Never shown to the player (AT-20).

**Example (ring 1, integer recursion, recomputed; the route is D.1's `[ASSUMED anchor]` route).** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Burden-free figures: this example and its table are the golden schedule without `U_BURDEN`, kept as the cross-check fixture of AC-BS-14a to AC-BS-16; the ruled sortie is the worked example under K1 to K5 below, where the tell opens 318 ticks into the carry.)* **Read the lit figure first: the player pushes noise, light and power at once. The lit reference sortie (beam on throughout, home ship lit and idle, `L0` 1678.5 AU) peaks at 3394.8 AU, 57 percent of `ATT_THRESHOLD`, at the end of the carry. The 29 percent figure below is the noise term alone (`I_light = I_power = 0`, `L0 = B`): a lower bound that omits two of the three terms and must not be read as the push.** The reference sortie pushes walk-in 312 ticks at 1, two door attempts (QUIET, 60 idle ticks), the cut 240 ticks at `U_TOOL` plus `U_LOUD`, the pickup, the carry out 624 ticks at 1, the console. Raw pushed: `312 + (240 * 4 + 180) + 624` = **2076 AU**, of which the cut is **1140 AU** (19 percent of `ATT_THRESHOLD`). From `L = B`: peak **1756.6 AU** (29 percent of `ATT_THRESHOLD`, 37 percent of `theta_HOLD`) at the end of the carry, then idle decay. Rows (the **golden-fixture schedule**, in order and in ticks: walk-in 312 at 1 AU, door attempts 60 idle, the cut 240 at `U_TOOL` plus `U_LOUD` on the last tick, the pickup TAP 12 idle (a fixture dwell, not an owner's duration), the carry 624 at 1 AU, the console approach 30 idle; exact results 1,756,600 mAU peak and 1,732,482 mAU after the console from `L0 = B`; 3,394,795 and 3,374,965 mAU with the beam and home terms from 1,678,501 mAU. Omitting the 12-tick pickup dwell moves the noise-only peak to 1,762,817 mAU, so the figures are exact only for this schedule):

| Scenario | Peak `L` | Share of `ATT_THRESHOLD` | Share of `theta_HOLD` |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Reference sortie, lit: noise plus the beam (288) plus the lit idle home ship (766 mAU/tick), from `L0` 1678.5 AU (home idle steady state)** | **3394.8 AU** | **57 percent** | **71 percent** |
| Reference sortie, noise term only, `L0 = B` 300 AU (lower bound; omits `I_light` and `I_power`) | 1756.6 AU | 29 percent | 37 percent |
| Three cuts in one room with 3-tile walks between, with beam and home terms | 4741.7 AU | 79 percent | 99 percent |
| Same, noise term only (lower bound) | 3165.2 AU | 53 percent | 66 percent |

Cuts to trip: chaining cut-and-walk cycles (240 ticks, one LOUD, 72 walking ticks), the tenth cycle's report crosses `ATT_THRESHOLD` on noise alone (3120 ticks, 52 s); with the beam and the home term the **fifth** does (1560 ticks, 26 s). Continuous running from rest reaches it in 2820 ticks (47.0 s) on noise alone and in 1476 ticks (24.6 s) with beam and home terms, which reproduces the Attention GDD's own scenario row (F-25: "crosses `theta` from idle in 1,476 ticks (24.6 s)"). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **Pre-ruling history, kept for the record:** the figures from here to the end of this paragraph describe the sortie without the `BURDEN` term; under the ruling the lit reference sortie meets a tell 318 ticks into the carry (the derivation below). *Finding (RK-A, C-BS-13; revised at stage 12 after the CD-GDD-ALIGN concern):* at the placeholders the lit reference sortie peaks at 57 percent of `ATT_THRESHOLD` and opens no tell, and a chain of cuts with the beam on does within half a minute, so "rising Attention" at the MVP comes from the lit job, running and chained cuts and not from a single cut. **A sweep of the three intake constants this formula reads over the lit golden schedule (`U_TOOL` 2 to 6, `U_LOUD` 30 x `U_TOOL` to 400, `TAU_ATT_TICKS` 1200 to 1816, the registry's safe range, 1817 failing Attention's abortability F-15; same integer recursion, run-quantised) never reaches `ATT_THRESHOLD`: the extreme corner (`U_TOOL` 6, `U_LOUD` 400, `TAU_ATT_TICKS` 1816) peaks at 3884 AU (65 percent), and at `TAU_ATT_TICKS` 1800 the best corner peaks at 3865 AU (64 percent). A lit listen of 300 or 600 ticks before the walk-in adds only 40 or 74 AU to the peak.** *(Re-swept 2026-10-27, consistency pass: the earlier sweep ran to `TAU_ATT_TICKS` 3600, outside the registry's safe range.)* `[UNV: the sweep is this GDD's own recomputation, regenerated by the headless fixture; Attention to confirm]`. So no retune of `U_TOOL`, `U_LOUD` or `TAU_ATT_TICKS` alone makes the reference sortie meet a tell; Attention's other levers (`NOISE_PER_TICK`, `W_L`, `W_P`, `B_table`, `ATT_THRESHOLD`) must move with them, or the route and node density (Derelict Authoring) must grow. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(the choice was a three-owner user decision, OQ-BS-27, and is now ruled: the derivation is in the paragraphs below)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* The choice of lever was Attention's (ASK-BS-A1, widened to these constants and made a precondition of the MVP playtest); the **outcome it must achieve is Boarding's design target X-BS-11** (Game Feel), so the 29 percent figure can no longer be read as the whole push and the MVP playtest cannot test a sortie that has no breach rhythm. The envelope rows assume the lit home ship's term keeps feeding the player's level while the crew is aboard, as the F-25 rows do `[UNV: Attention to confirm]`. The closed-form cross-check (`E(t+dt) = E(t) e^(-dt/tau) + r tau (1 - e^(-dt/tau))`) gives about 1467 AU of excess at the end of the carry against the recursion's 1456.6 AU (1756.6 less `B`), inside the quantisation.

*(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* **The ruling, and what it can and cannot settle (OQ-BS-27, option B; X3, D71, D49, D50).** The design review showed that the reference decision needs three properties together: (i) *crossing*, (ii) *causality* (the cut and the carry supply at least about 50 percent of the headroom above `L0`, so the tell is the player's doing: 28 percent of the threshold is home idle and is not a cause) and (iii) *a live choice*. The user ruled option B: Attention adds a carry-state intake (registered as `U_BURDEN`, about 7,000 mAU per tick) while a carried instance is held, the decision-bearing carry is lengthened to about 21 to 28 tiles (D.1), freezing with cargo is priced, and Attention recalibrates its weights and `ATT_THRESHOLD` so that an ordinary sortie reaches the threshold (D50, lever 1; Attention derives it). *Result (Attention F-26, re-review):* Attention ran the recalibration and it does not close with the weights alone, so `W_L`, `W_P`, `W_S`, `B_table`, `TAU_ATT_TICKS` and `ATT_THRESHOLD` are unchanged and the burden term carries the crossing; under delegated ruling R5 the burden is scoped to a salvage carry and the decay is suspended while it is held, and no recalibration is pending *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*. On D49 neither abortability retune is made now: the switches-only guarantee and the advisory transient lint stand, and are revisited after the recalibration.

*Reading of the ruling (confirmed by the owner).* The sheet words the intake as 'while carrying a HEAVY item'. This GDD applies it **class-blind** (any carried instance): the reference payoff `htr_a` is MEDIUM (12,000 g against `WEIGHT_HEAVY_G` 25,000 [PLACEHOLDER]), so a HEAVY-only term would never fire on the reference sortie, and a class-keyed term is a mass term in all but name, which option B itself rules out (IDB-21); the decision text (D71) carries no HEAVY qualifier. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* **Confirmed (re-review):** Attention keys the `BURDEN` push on a salvage carry of any class and any mass (AT-3, F-26; AC-AT-06f pushes the same for a 6 kg and a 90 kg item), so `htr_a` pushes 42,000 mAU per held run standing and 48,000 moving (O-6, AC-BS-127). The registry is class-blind as of 2026-10-28 (revised 2026-10-28): `carry_burden_intake` and `U_BURDEN` read 'a salvage carry ... any class' and `noise_push` carries no HEAVY term, so no registry request is outstanding *(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review)*.

*Derivation.* The burden `U_BURDEN`, the carry length `N` tiles and the level at pickup `L_p` must satisfy these constraints together. Attention's discrete integrator governs, and with the decay suspended on every run that holds salvage (delegated ruling R5 (a)) the carry is linear: `L(t) = L_p + r_tot t`, run-quantised to 54,324 mAU per run, with `r_tot = 1000 x U_BURDEN + 1000 + 288 + 766` = 9,054 mAU per tick (the burden, the carry locomotion noise, the beam and the lit idle home ship), so `t_x = 6 x ceil((1000 x ATT_THRESHOLD - L_p) / 54,324)`. **One convention, Attention's:** the golden walk-in is Attention's fixed 312 ticks and its run accounting is canonical (R5); the scaled walk-in `24 N` of the earlier table is withdrawn. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*

- **K1, crossing inside the carry:** `L` reaches `ATT_THRESHOLD` at `t_x` ticks into the carry with `0 < t_x < 48 N`, and the clamped level reaches `L_CEIL` before the console press. The earlier 'peak >= 1.05 x `ATT_THRESHOLD`' could never hold on the clamped level (`L_CEIL_PM` 1020), and the shadow-trace margin that replaced it is withdrawn: with the decay suspended while salvage is held the unclamped trace only rises (to about 2.6 x `ATT_THRESHOLD` at the console of the 28-tile carry) and measures nothing (Attention OQ-AT-34 (2)). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*
- **K2, causality:** at the tick the tell opens, the cut and the carry supply at least 50 percent of the headroom `ATT_THRESHOLD - L0` = 4,321,499 mAU: with their pushes replaced by idle, the level at that tick is at most `ATT_THRESHOLD - 0.5 x (ATT_THRESHOLD - L0)` (Attention F-26 measures it the same way). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*
- **K3, a live choice, as a margin over the dwell sweep:** at the tick the tell opens the carry still ahead is `rem = 48 N - t_x`, and the **live share** is `live_pm = max(0, min(1000, (rem - TELL_DRAW_LO) x 1000 div (TELL_DRAW_HI - TELL_DRAW_LO)))`, the permille of the tell draw for which `fits_carry` (D.3) is false and walking on loses to the breach (Attention F-26 reports the same share). K3's margin is `live_pm_min`, the minimum of `live_pm` over the four lit pre-lift dwell variants of `REF_GOLDEN` (0, 300, 900 and 1800 ticks between the cut's completion and the lift, EC-BS-66), and K3 passes when `live_pm_min >= LIVE_PM_MIN`. `LIVE_PM_MIN` is the boarding spike's pass line `[PLACEHOLDER — set at the boarding spike, AC-BS-132]`; this GDD's provisional design value is 250 permille (the earlier `F_LIVE` 0.25, `rem >= 1,125`), a placeholder reference that the ruled band cannot meet (the best in-band point is 140 permille at zero dwell and 66 over the sweep); the spike's play-set value replaces it (AC-BS-132) and AC-BS-123 (d) is judged only against that value *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*
- **K4, restraint without the carry:** variants in which the carry intake does not run (cut and leave the module on the deck; listen and leave) stay at or below 0.95 `theta_HOLD`. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **K5, freezing is priced:** with the instance held the level never falls under `theta_HOLD`, so a tell can be outwaited only after a drop. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* With the decay suspended while salvage is held the level never falls at all (R5 (a)) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*.

*Worked example* (the lit reference schedule of the golden file with the carry scaled to `N` tiles: walk-in 312 ticks at 1 AU (Attention's fixed convention), two door attempts 60 idle, the cut 240 with `U_LOUD` on the last tick, pickup dwell 12, carry `48 N` ticks at `U_BURDEN` 7 with the decay suspended, console approach 30 holding; beam 288 and home 766 mAU per tick throughout; from `L0` 1,678,501 mAU; Attention's run-quantised integer recursion, which reproduces the burden-free golden peak 3,394,795 exactly): *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*

| `N` (tiles) | carry ticks | `L_p` (mAU) | `t_x` into the carry | tell opens at tick | carry still ahead at `t_x` (`rem`) | live share `live_pm` (K3, permille) | clamped peak / threshold *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)* |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 13 (pre-ruling anchor) | 624 | 3,144,759 | 318 | 942 | 306 | 0 | 1.02 (`L_CEIL`) |
| 21 | 1008 | 3,144,759 | 318 | 942 | 690 | 0 | 1.02 |
| 24 (band sample) | 1152 | 3,144,759 | 318 | 942 | 834 | 0 | 1.02 |
| 28 (reference carry, DA-48) | 1344 | 3,144,759 | 318 | 942 | 1,026 | 140 | 1.02 |

(The live share is K3's `live_pm`, in permille: `fits_carry` is false when the carry ahead exceeds the drawn tell length `T`. The 28-tile figures are Attention's F-26 figures, quoted.) Reading it: *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*

- **K1 holds** at every `N`: the tell opens 318 ticks into the carry, 51, 32, 28 and 24 percent of the way at 13, 21, 24 and 28 tiles, and the level reaches `L_CEIL` within two more runs. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*
- **K2 holds**: the tell opens at schedule tick 942 at every `N`, and with the cut's and the carry's pushes replaced by idle the level there is 2,092,108 mAU, so the cut and the carry supply (6,000,000 - 2,092,108) / 4,321,499 = 90 percent of the headroom (Attention F-26). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*
- **K3 is thin, and it is a spike gate.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)* The tell opens 318 ticks into the carry whatever `N` is, because that tick is set by `U_BURDEN` and `L_p`, so the carry still ahead grows one for one with `N`: 28 tiles is the only point of the ruled band with any live share (140 permille), 21 and 24 tiles give none, and any live share at all needs a carry of at least 1,219 ticks (25.4 tiles), the provisional 250 permille at least 1,443 ticks (30.1 tiles), beyond the ruled band (Attention F-26). *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* Derelict Authoring builds the 28-tile end: its earlier provisional lever n = 9 (R = 1,176 px, 24.5 tiles) is 43 px under the 1,219 floor and gives `live_pm` 0 at every dwell, so it is withdrawn, and DA-48 now proposes n = 12 with the cut point 24 px deeper in `a_machine` (R = 744 + 48 x 12 + 24 = 1,344 px, 228 px of it inside `a_machine`, under the 336 px cap). Lever sweep at the four lit pre-lift dwells (`live_pm = floor((Lc - t_x - 900) x 1000 / 900)`, `t_x` 318, 336, 360, 384): `Lc` 1,176 gives 0/0/0/0; 1,224 gives 6/0/0/0; 1,272 gives 60/40/13/0; 1,320 (n = 12, d = 0) gives 113/93/66/40, minimum 40; 1,344 gives 140/120/93/66, minimum 66. Only the 1,344 px route gives the 66-permille floor quoted above. No `U_BURDEN` inside its boot range 7 to 10 meets 250 permille at 28 tiles (10 gives 226; about 11,500 mAU per tick would), and raising `L_p` is not a remedy: cut-and-leave tracks `L_p`, so a pickup level high enough for K3 breaks K4. **Pre-lift dwell sweep** (lit, 28 tiles, FIXTURE-312; the yield lies on the deck while the player waits in `a_spine`, and the decay runs because nothing is held; the printed sweep treats the wait as idle and `REF_GOLDEN` regenerates it with the walk) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*: waits of 0, 300, 600, 900 and 1800 ticks give `L_p` 3,144,759, 2,999,045, 2,875,735, 2,771,386 and 2,545,111 mAU, `t_x` 318, 336, 348, 360 and 384, `rem` 1,026, 1,008, 996, 984 and 960, and `live_pm` 140, 120, 106, 93 and 66, so `live_pm_min` over the K3 sweep (0, 300, 900, 1800) is **66 permille**; a lit wait of any length floors at 26 permille (`L_p` tends to the lit derelict steady state, about 2.2 million mAU), while a 1800-tick wait in the dark (beam and home lamps off, the pre-glow dark floor of 294 mAU per tick) gives `L_p` 1,685,287, `t_x` 480, `rem` 864 and no live share (EC-BS-66). Because the burden suspends the decay, the pause between the cut and the lift costs the margin slowly and does not erase it at ordinary pacing (these sweep figures are this GDD's recomputation with Attention's integrator; `REF_GOLDEN` is the authority). **Resolved 2026-10-27 — delegated ruling R5 (re-review; user delegated to best judgment):** of the three options the re-review put (A, an Attention-owned tell lever, the tell range or the tell onset relative to the lift, checked jointly against K1 and K4; B, accept a thin live share as the MVP state and set K3 as a pass line on it; C, relax the ruled 28-tile cap), this GDD takes **B**: the thin share is the MVP state on paper, AC-BS-123 (d) is a spike gate owned by the boarding spike (AC-BS-132), which sets `LIVE_PM_MIN` from play, and option A is the spike's candidate lever, Attention's to evaluate; option C is pre-ruled in conditional form by delegated ruling R19 of 2026-10-28 (OQ-BS-34): if the spike fails at every lever the X3 band's upper end widens to 31 tiles for the reference sortie and the lower end stays 21. This GDD sets no weights.
- **K4 holds**: cut and leave, lit (the module left on the deck and the walk out unladen at `V_WALK` 2), peaks at 3,280,601, 3,353,201, 3,378,491 and 3,410,670 mAU at 13, 21, 24 and 28 tiles, which is 0.68, 0.70, 0.70 and 0.71 of `theta_HOLD` (4,800,000); listen and leave peaks at 2,377,946 mAU (0.50). **The beam-off and home-lamps-off variants cross, and they must**: the burden is about seven times the light terms, so dimming the lamp moves the crossing and does not prevent it (at 28 tiles the tell opens 348 ticks into the carry with the beam off, 432 with the home lamps dark from the 829,000 mAU dark start, and 468 with both; Attention F-26). That is what 'carrying is a stimulus the player chooses' means: restraint is in the lift, the drop and the leaving, not in the lamp. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*
- **K5 holds**: a holder never gets under `theta_HOLD` and, with the decay suspended, the level never falls at all, so freezing with cargo does not abort the tell (standing still with the cargo after the cut crosses 360 ticks after the pickup, and from the lit-idle level with no cut 540; Attention E-66). After a drop at the tell's opening (`L` 6,023,931 mAU) the abort completes **810 ticks** later standing still lit (the beam and the lit home ship, 1,054 mAU per tick; Attention's figure), 714 with the beam off and the home ship lit, and 600 at the pre-glow dark floor of 294 mAU per tick (this GDD's recomputation with Attention's integrator; `REF_GOLDEN` is the authority); the hand oracle O-4 (594 from a level seeded at `theta`, 630 from `L_CEIL`, dark) is the same rule from a seeded start. The docked pod's status glow adds its term to the floor wherever its section is loaded (BS-19), so the dark figures are snapshots and the live code is the authority. A freeze after the cut with the module still on the deck is not an escape from a tell, because the tell is the carry's and has not opened; it is the pre-lift dwell of K3, legitimate restraint priced in time (EC-BS-66). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*

**Fixture convention** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* (R5: Attention's walk-in convention and tick accounting are canonical). Every K1 to K5 figure, the O-1 to O-6 oracles, the lever sweep and the worked-example table are computed on Attention's lit golden FIXTURE (F-26): a walk-in of 312 ticks at 1 AU whatever `N`, two door attempts (60 idle), the cut 240 ticks with `U_LOUD` on the last tick, the pickup dwell 12, the carry `48 N` ticks at `U_BURDEN` 7 with the decay suspended, the console approach 30. It is not a replay of the built route: the built 28-tile route's walk-in is `ceil(1,344 / V_WALK)` = 672 ticks (732 with the two door attempts, D.1), 360 ticks longer than the fixture, so a `REF_GOLDEN` regenerated on the built route will not reproduce the printed pickup level, `t_x` or `live_pm`. The printed 140/120/93/66 permille are fixture values; the main review estimates about 180/160/120/80 on the built route ([UNV], not recomputed here, not an oracle). `REF_GOLDEN` governs and AC-BS-132 sets the pass line against it. Fixture-tied rows are labelled FIXTURE-312.

**Acceptance shape (re-derived under the ruling).** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)* One generated golden file, **`REF_GOLDEN`**, is produced by running the headless reference schedule against Attention's real integrator at the live constants (the decay suspended on every run that drains a `BURDEN` push), with the pod glow's term read from the live emitters. It replaces every hard-coded placeholder figure in the sortie's acceptance rows (AC-BS-6(e), 56, 122; AC-BS-14a, 14b, 15 and 16 are kept as the burden-free cross-check fixture, below). It records, for the lit schedule and for each variant, the per-run `L`, the clamped peak, `tell_open_tick` (or none), `t_x`, `rem`, `live_pm` and the `phase` at the console press and at the install. **Variants:** cut and leave; listen and leave; beam off; home lamps off; both off; short dwell (no listen, no pickup dwell); **pre-lift dwell** of 0, 300, 900 and 1800 ticks lit and 1800 ticks dark between the cut's completion and the lift; **drop-outwait-retake** (drop at `tell_open_tick`, stand still lit until the abort, re-take the same item at once and walk the rest of the carry); and the **home leg** (the console press, the pod leg run-quantised to 948 ticks, the 486-tick home carry, the install). The assertions are deterministic margins on that one file: (a) *crossing*: the lit run's `tell_open_tick` lies inside the carry and the clamped level reaches `L_CEIL` before the console press (K1); (b) *restraint*: the no-carry variants (cut and leave; listen and leave) stay at or below 0.95 x `theta_HOLD`; a drop at `tell_open_tick` followed by stillness aborts the tell, and stillness with the instance held never does; the beam-off, home-lamps-off and both-off variants are **expected to cross** and are asserted to cross no earlier than the lit run, in the order lit, beam off, home lamps off, both off (K4); (c) *causality*: with the cut's and the carry's pushes replaced by idle, the level at the lit run's `tell_open_tick` is at most `ATT_THRESHOLD - 0.5 x (ATT_THRESHOLD - L0)` (K2); (d) *a live choice* (a spike gate, not story-ready as pass/fail until the boarding spike sets `LIVE_PM_MIN`, AC-BS-132): `live_pm_min` over the four lit pre-lift dwell variants is at least `LIVE_PM_MIN` (K3); until then the file reports `live_pm` for every variant (today 140, 120, 93 and 66 permille at 28 tiles); (e) *the home leg*: on the reference carry the tell opened in the derelict is resolved before the pod arrives (`rem` + 30 + 948 exceeds `TELL_DRAW_HI` 1800), so at the install the `phase` is neither TELL nor BREACH, no `BURDEN` push is drained after the seat enters the pod, and `L < theta_HOLD` (today 4,389,820 mAU with the beam and the lit home ship feeding the level in the pod, 3,796,361 with no pod intake; EC-BS-63); (f) *drop-outwait-retake*: re-taking the same item after the abort ends the rearm (Attention AT-10, the item-keyed rearm) and a tell re-opens on the resumed carry (today 156 ticks after the re-take, `rem` 870, `live_pm` 0; EC-BS-67); (g) *the class-blind push*: with `carry_ref` = `htr_a` (MEDIUM) one held run pushes 42,000 mAU standing and 48,000 moving, the same as `scr_a` (HEAVY) (O-6). The earlier (b), which kept the beam-off and home-lamps-off variants at or below 0.95 x `theta_HOLD`, and the earlier shadow-trace margin of (a) are withdrawn. **Burden-free cross-check fixture:** AC-BS-14a, 14b, 15 and 16 run the same integrator with `U_BURDEN` set to 0 and are asserted against an independent hand recursion, as an integrator check and not as the sortie's expectation. Independent of the file, **oracle cases** are computed by hand and are never read from it: **O-1**, one run holding the cut-completion tick from `L = B` 300,000 mAU: `I_run` = 1000 x 4 x 6 + 1000 x 180 = 204,000 mAU, so `L'` = 504,000 mAU; **O-2**, one idle run from there: `ceil(204,000 x 6 / 1800)` = 680, so `L'` = 503,320 mAU; **O-3**, `L = B` with no intake leaves `L = B`; **O-4**, the abort ticks of AC-BS-18 as the function `t_abort(r_floor)` of D.3 at the live dark floor `r_floor`: 594 from a level seeded at `theta` and 630 from `L_CEIL` at the pre-glow floor of 294 mAU per tick (the pod glow raises `r_floor`, and the oracle is recomputed by hand from the live value); **O-5**, one carry run from `L = B` with the beam and home terms off: `I_run` = 6 x (1000 + 1000 x `U_BURDEN`) = 48,000 mAU at `U_BURDEN` 7 and `dec` = 0 (a `BURDEN` run), so `L'` = 348,000 mAU; and **O-6**, one held standing run from `L = B` with the beam and home terms off: `I_run` = 6 x 7,000 = 42,000 mAU and `dec` = 0, so `L'` = 342,000 mAU, the same for `htr_a` (MEDIUM) and `scr_a` (HEAVY).

### D.3 `tell_window_fit`

The tell_window_fit formula is defined as:

`carry_tiles(T) = floor(T * V_CARRY / TILE_PX)` ; `run_tiles(T) = floor(T * V_RUN / TILE_PX)` ; `T_after_cut = T - SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS` ; `fits_carry(px, T) <=> px <= T * V_CARRY` ; `t_abort(L_cancel) = t_decay(L_cancel) + ABORT_HOLD_TICKS - GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` (the first run under the line counts as run 1, AT-10, so the abort fires on the 20th run under `theta_HOLD`) ; `partial_alive <=> t_abort <= PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS` (inclusive, IA-16)

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `T` | int ticks | `TELL_DRAW_LO` 900 to `TELL_DRAW_HI` 1800 played [PLACEHOLDER]; `TELL_MIN_TICKS` 600 (derived) only via `attention_arm_tell` and fixtures | Attention AT-8, AT-21 | the tell time remaining when the carry (or the cut) begins; a tell at entry has `tell_len` in [900, 1800] |
| `px` | int px | D.1 legs | Layout / DA `[ASSUMED anchor]` | the carry route to the console |
| `V_CARRY`, `V_RUN`, `TILE_PX` | int | 1, 3, 48 | registry (locked) | gait speeds and tile size |
| `SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS` | int ticks | 240 [PLACEHOLDER] | Interaction | a cut begun at tell entry leaves `T - 240` |
| `t_decay(L_cancel)` | int ticks | 480 from `theta`, 516 from `L_CEIL` | recomputed from `attention_integrator` with the dark floor 294 mAU/tick (AT F-16); Attention's `abortability_play` gives 540 to `theta_HOLD - ATT_MARGIN` from `L_CEIL` at ring 1 | the stillness-and-dark time to fall under `theta_HOLD` |
| `ABORT_HOLD_TICKS` | int ticks | 120 (60 to 300) [PLACEHOLDER] | Attention AT-10 | consecutive time under `theta_HOLD` that aborts the tell |
| `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS` | int ticks | 600 (180 to 1200) [PLACEHOLDER] | Interaction IA-16 | how long a cancelled cut keeps its progress |

**Output Range:** booleans and tile counts per `T`; `carry_tiles` 12 to 37, `run_tiles` 37 to 112 over `T` 600 to 1800. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* With a HEAVY item stowed run is disabled (user ruling D18), so replace `V_RUN` by `V_WALK` 2 in `run_tiles`: 25, 37, 50 and 75 tiles at `T` 600, 900, 1200 and 1800.

**Example:**

| `T` (tell ticks remaining) | carry tiles | run tiles | `T_after_cut` | carry tiles after a cut | run tiles after a cut |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 600 (fixture floor, `TELL_MIN_TICKS`) | 12 | 37 | 360 | 7 | 22 |
| 900 (shortest played, `TELL_DRAW_LO`) | 18 | 56 | 660 | 13 | 41 |
| 1200 | 25 | 75 | 960 | 20 | 60 |
| 1800 (longest, `TELL_DRAW_HI`) | 37 | 112 | 1560 | 32 | 97 |

(Pre-ruling anchor and fixture arithmetic; the ruled decision is the liveness paragraph below.) `htr_a` (13.0 tiles, 624 ticks, `[ASSUMED anchor]`): a tell that opens as the cut begins, at the shortest played draw, leaves 660 ticks after the cut, so the carry fits with 36 ticks to spare; at the 600-tick fixture floor it leaves 360 and does not fit, while dropping the module and running (208 ticks) always fits. `scr_a` (12.0 tiles, 576 ticks) fits by 84. The fit means *reach the console before the tell expires*; the hop stays legal inside a BREACH (WM-26), so a late arrival is a hazard and not a refusal. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* A hop from a derelict locus also **voids the due-run breach** when every seat recorded at tell entry has left by then (AT-22, user ruling D46): reaching the console and pressing it before `breach_due_T` turns the tell into an aborted one with the module still in the arms, and a hop after the breach has opened ends the breach (BS-13 b); a home-ship locus is not voidable. **Stillness and the partial:** a player who cancels the cut at `theta` and goes dark and still falls under `theta_HOLD` after 480 ticks, and the tell aborts after `480 + 120 - 6` = **594 ticks**, six ticks inside the inclusive `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`; from `L_CEIL` it is `516 + 120 - 6` = **630 ticks**, thirty past it (Attention's F-15b adds the full `ABORT_HOLD_TICKS` and gets 660 from `L_CEIL` with its margin). So a cancelled cut's partial survives an abort-by-stillness only at the lowest entry levels and only if the cut is resumed within six ticks of the abort; otherwise the cut restarts from zero. That is Attention's "restraint usually costs the partial" (AT-10) arrived at through the ledger rather than the abort. A cut in progress is loud and keeps `L` above `theta_HOLD`, so the abort is forfeited for as long as the cut runs (AT-10). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* **Liveness and the price of freezing, as ruled (OQ-BS-27, option B).** *What the table above says.* The fit table is the pre-ruling decision and it was dead: `htr_a` (13 tiles) fits every played draw. *What the ruling changes.* The tell now opens `t_x` ticks into the carry (D.2: 318 ticks, about 6.6 tiles, at `U_BURDEN` 7 with the decay suspended *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*), so the quantity that decides the choice is the carry still ahead at that tick, `px_ahead = 48 N - t_x`, and `fits_carry(px_ahead, T)` is false when `px_ahead > T x V_CARRY`. The measure of a live choice is K3 of D.2: the live share `live_pm` over the pre-lift dwell sweep against the spike's `LIVE_PM_MIN`. Worked at 21, 24 and 28 tiles it gives a carry ahead of 690, 834 and 1,026 ticks and live shares of 0, 0 and 140 permille, so the choice is live in kind and thin in margin, and the pass line is the boarding spike's (AC-BS-132, delegated ruling R5) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*. **Where the tell opens** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: 318 ticks is about 6.6 tiles into the carry, and which room that is depends on Derelict Authoring's placement of the lengthened route (OQ-BS-31), and on DA-48's geometry it lies 90 to 156 px past the centre of `door_a_spine_machine` (computed at EC-BS-68); the level-designer's reading of the earlier onset put it on or just past `door_a_spine_machine`, where a drop on a door cell cannot land (EC-BS-12) and an open door conducts the cold (EC-BS-20), so this GDD asks Derelict Authoring for a **decision reach** where the tell is expected to open: at least 3 tiles wide, no door cells, outside the cold projection, and lit or with a stick shelf (ASK-BS-D4 (d), EC-BS-68). *Freezing is priced, and the price is the drop.* With the instance held, the carry-state intake keeps `L` above `theta_HOLD` whether the player walks or stands (the net intake stays positive and `L` sits at `L_CEIL`), so a held module never lets the tell abort; to outwait it the player must `drop` first, and the abort then completes 810 ticks after a drop at the tell's opening if the player stays lit (Attention's figure) and 600 at the pre-glow dark floor of 294 mAU per tick (the O-4 hand oracle gives 594 from a level seeded at `theta` and 630 from `L_CEIL`; AC-BS-18); the docked pod's status glow (ON while docked, SYSTEM-switched, its footprint limited to the console cell, BS-19) adds to the floor wherever it is loaded, so the live code is the authority *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. A freeze after the cut with the module on the deck is not an escape from a tell, because no tell has opened: the tell is the carry's. The earlier cheap freeze (about 50 runs x 19 mSU = 940 mSU = 0.94 percent of `SAN_MAX`, D.8) therefore buys a lapse only for a tell that the carry opened and the player then dropped, and it costs the carry already walked and a re-take under light. **Drop, outwait, re-take** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*: the rearm keys on the item (Attention AT-10), so re-taking the same module after the abort ends the rearm at once, and the tell re-opens about 156 ticks into the resumed carry (`rem` 870 at 28 tiles, inside every played draw); the player has bought a carry that fits for about 810 ticks of lit stillness plus Sanity's dwell rate, which is restraint priced in time and not a cheap freeze (EC-BS-67). *Escalation:* while `L < theta_HOLD` the escalation clock is frozen (user ruling X4, Attention's behaviour-keyed rule), so after the drop the tell bed does not keep climbing against a player who has gone still, and Audio's tiers follow the frozen escalation. *Re-keyed draws:* the tell length `T` is drawn once at entry (AT-8) from a stream that is re-keyed per restore epoch (user ruling X8, D16; epoch rules R13/R14, Save/Load), so a quit or a death cannot steer the draw. *Not hashed, not persisted.*

### D.4 `hazard_dwell_budget`

The hazard_dwell_budget formula is defined as:

`dwell = ceil(px_in / V_WALK) + T_open + n_cuts * SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS + T_scan + px_out / V_CARRY` ; `margin_pm = floor(1000 * dwell / T_crit)` ; for the unbreathable branch `traverse_runs = ceil(round_trip_ticks / GRAPH_STEP_TICKS)` ; `reserve_cost_mgu = traverse_runs * R_BREATH` ; `reserve_left_mgu = max(0, arrival_reserve - reserve_cost_mgu)` ; `reserve_pm = floor(1000 * reserve_cost_mgu / arrival_reserve)` with the guard `arrival_reserve = 0 -> reserve_pm = 1000, everything unserved` (G5; boundary AC in AC-BS-106) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review, Suit R6 pod derivation)* (the former `reserve_pm` over `RESERVE_S * TICK_RATE`, 497 at 56 tiles, is deleted except as a label: the share of a FULL reserve, the stale reading) ; `T_crit` is DA D.10's `runs_critical * GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` (cited, not re-derived)

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `px_in`, `px_out` | int px | up to 7 tiles x 48 = 336 inside the cold room | DA-28 (the crossing bound); reference 228 px each way = 204 + `REF_CARRY_CUT_OFFSET_PX` d 24 (DA-48 proposal, [PLACEHOLDER — derive at spike]); 204 px is the pre-ruling 13-tile anchor *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* | the in-room legs, walked in and carried out |
| `T_open` | int ticks | 0 or 30 | Interaction IA-24 (`DOOR_ATTEMPT_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER]) | opening `door_a_spine_machine` from outside costs no cold; count 0 if the sill is not crossed |
| `n_cuts` | int | 1 to 8 | the player | cuts made in one visit |
| `T_scan` | int ticks | 0 or 120 | Interaction (`FIELD_CHECK_TICKS` [PLACEHOLDER]) | a SCAN taken inside |
| `T_crit` | int ticks | 2184 (364 runs, CRITICAL at `T0` -22000 mC); FAILED at 6546 (1091 runs) | DA D.10 / Suit D.4 | the cold clock at the room's authored temperature |
| `round_trip_ticks` | int ticks | up to 1,632 (34 tiles carried both ways = Suit's `VACUUM_BUDGET_RUNS` after two legs; DA D.7 conformed, see the DA request); the former 2,688 (56 tiles) is withdrawn *(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review, Suit R6 pod derivation)* | Suit D.11 / AC-SE-75a (DA-28 and D.7 conform) | a carried round trip through an unbreathable stretch |
| `RESERVE_S` | int s | 90 (63 to 240; narrowed from 45 to 240 by Suit's A15) [PLACEHOLDER] *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* | Suit | the engaged reserve breath; the reserve at the airlock is `RESERVE_S` x 3000 mGU less the outbound pod leg *(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review, Suit R6 pod derivation)* |
| `arrival_reserve` | int mGU | [222,600, 222,900] at the defaults ([221,100, 221,400] on the 975-tick slack leg) | Suit D.1 'Pod leg derivation' (QUOTED, NOT re-derived): `max(0, RESERVE_MAX_MGU 270,000 - d x N)` with d = `R_BREATH` 300 mGU per run, N = 158 / 157 runs on the nominal 945-tick leg, 47,100 to 47,400 mGU = 17.4 percent | the reserve at the airlock; it drains in the pod because the statuses run there (D60, R6) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review, Suit R6 pod derivation)* |
| `R_BREATH` | int mGU per run | 300 | Suit | the reserve spent per 6-tick run while breath_src is RESERVE |
| `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` | int ticks | 6 | Life-Support Graph | ticks per run |
| `RESERVE_WARN_MGU` | int mGU | 90,000 | Suit | the warn edge (`RESERVE_WARN_S` 30 s x 3000) |

**Output Range:** `dwell` from `SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS` (240, a cut with no crossing) to `dwell_fixed + n_cuts * SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS` (2262 for eight cuts at the defaults, where `dwell_fixed` = 342 = 114 + 0 + 228 without a SCAN) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; `margin_pm` above 1000 means CRITICAL is reached. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review, Suit R6 pod derivation)*: `reserve_pm` is 1000 with everything unserved for `arrival_reserve` 0 (the guard; the Suit range floor of `RESERVE_S` 63 leaves an arrival reserve of 63 x 3000 - 47,400 = 141,600 to 141,900 (63 x 3000 - 47,100) on a nominal leg, so 0 cannot occur there, but a long leg, a low reserve or an injected route can) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* and `T_crit` must be positive (DA D.10 guarantees it). A route with `margin_pm >= 1000` for its authored crossing is a Derelict Authoring lint failure, not a Boarding rule. The Graph's room cools and the gain rises over a long visit, so these margins shrink; they are exact only at `T0`.

**Example:** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, recomputed at the built route, G4)* `htr_a` on the built route (DA-48 n = 12, d = 24: in-room leg 204 + 24 = 228 px each way): `ceil(228 / 2) + 0 + 240 + 0 + 228` = `114 + 0 + 240 + 0 + 228` = **582 ticks = 9.7 s = 266 permille** (`floor(1000 x 582 / 2184)`) of `T_crit` 2184. With one SCAN: 702 (321 permille). The pre-ruling anchor (204 px, `102 + 0 + 240 + 0 + 204`) gave 546 ticks = 9.1 s = 250 permille, 666 with a SCAN, and 2226 for eight cuts. Worst case inside DA's 7-tile bound with a SCAN: `168 + 0 + 240 + 120 + 336` = **864 ticks = 14.4 s = 395 permille** (`floor(1000 * 864 / 2184)`), leaving 1320 ticks (22 s). Each further cut in the visit adds 240: `dwell(n) = 342 + 240 n` gives 1062 (49 percent, 486 permille) for three, 1542 (71 percent, 706) for five, 2022 (93 percent, 925) for seven, and **2262 for eight, which exceeds 2184 by 78**: `a_machine` holds about eight module nodes (DA C.2), so stripping the room is lethal by construction; it tolerates one purposeful cut and a shuttle, not a strip. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *("eight cuts" holds only at the default `T_crit` 2184 and `SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS` 240. As a function of the live constants the lethal count is `n_lethal = floor((T_crit - dwell_fixed) / SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS) + 1`, which is 8 at the defaults (`floor((2184 - 342) / 240) + 1 = floor(7.675) + 1`) and moves with `A_MACHINE_T_MC`, `SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS` and the anchors. The statement "stripping `a_machine` is lethal" is therefore a lint on Derelict Authoring's side, `module_nodes(a_machine) >= n_lethal`, and not a constant of this GDD.)* The irony is the authored one (DA-28): `htr_a` is the heater that keeps `a_machine` survivable once the bus is woken, so cutting it removes the thing that would have made lingering safe.

*(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review, Suit R6 pod derivation)* **Unbreathable branch (B's `b_breach`).** The traverse is read against the reserve the player HAS (`arrival_reserve` [222,600, 222,900] at the defaults), carried both ways (the conservative reading); 8 runs = 2,400 mGU per tile of round trip; the warn edge is 90,000; `k0 = ceil((arrival - 90,000) / 300)` = 442 or 443.

| case | ticks | runs | cost mGU | left mGU | `reserve_pm` of arrival | state |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
