cut and leave; listen and leave; beam off; home lamps off; both off; short dwell; pre-lift dwell 0, 300, 900 and 1800 ticks lit and 1800 dark; drop-outwait-retake; the home leg) THEN D.2's acceptance shape holds as deterministic margins: (a) the lit run's `tell_open_tick` lies inside the carry and the clamped level reaches `L_CEIL` before the console press; (b) the cut-and-leave and listen-and-leave variants stay at or below 0.95 x `theta_HOLD`, a drop at `tell_open_tick` followed by stillness aborts the tell and stillness with the instance held never aborts it, and the beam-off, home-lamps-off and both-off variants cross no earlier than the lit run, in that order; (c) with the cut's and the carry's pushes replaced by idle, the level at the lit run's `tell_open_tick` is at most `ATT_THRESHOLD - 0.5 x (ATT_THRESHOLD - L0)`; (d) `live_pm_min` over the four lit pre-lift dwell variants is at least `LIVE_PM_MIN` (K3; judged only against `LIVE_PM_MIN`), on the reference carry of 1,344 px (Derelict Authoring DA-48); a `REF_GOLDEN` regenerated on a route under 1,219 px is a Derelict Authoring defect (Attention IR-4 ATT_CARRY_NOT_LIVE, AC-DA-148), not a K3 result *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; (e) on the reference carry, at the install the `phase` is neither TELL nor BREACH, no `BURDEN` push is drained after the seat enters the pod, and `L < theta_HOLD`; (f) in the drop-outwait-retake variant the re-take of the same item ends the rearm and a tell re-opens on the resumed carry; (g) one held run of `htr_a` (MEDIUM) pushes 42,000 mAU standing and 48,000 moving, the same as `scr_a` (HEAVY); and the hand-computed oracle cases O-1 (504,000 mAU), O-2 (503,320 mAU), O-3 (`L = B`), O-4 (`t_abort(r_floor)`: 594 and 630 ticks at the pre-glow floor of 294 mAU per tick), O-5 (348,000 mAU) and O-6 (342,000 mAU) hold independently of the file. Today's figures, FIXTURE-312 (Attention's lit golden fixture at the ruled 28-tile carry; D.2 fixture convention), for the record and not as the oracle *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*: (a) the tell opens at carry tick 318 and the peak is `L_CEIL`; (b) the cut-and-leave peak is 0.71 of `theta_HOLD` at 28 tiles and the four variants open at carry ticks 318, 348, 432 and 468; (c) the idle level at tick 942 is 2,092,108 mAU (90 percent of the headroom supplied); (d) `live_pm` 140, 120, 93 and 66 permille (minimum 66) at 28 tiles; (e) 4,389,820 mAU at the install; (f) the tell re-opens 156 ticks after the re-take. [D.2, D.3, EC-BS-63, EC-BS-66, EC-BS-67]

### I. The dark dock, content constraints and the clocks

- **AC-BS-51a** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *deleted by the ruling of OQ-BS-4*: it asserted the lit exemption (M1) or the reserved lamp charge (M3), neither of which is taken (IA-6 stands). The id is not reused.

### M. Playtest targets (manual; five testers, at least three outside the project; thresholds [PROVISIONAL: creative-director])

- **AC-BS-69 (manual)** X-BS-1: first sortie to A, median W_ABOARD (`a_dock` arrival to the console press, D.1) between 3 and 5 min [PROVISIONAL: creative-director] (the upper bound is 5 min and not 6: the median must stay below the sanity stage-1 point, 315.8 s, D.8; an 8 min median would equal `T_SORTIE_SAFE_TICKS`); no tester completes W_LOOP (home console press to install) under 90 s; the observer also records the share of W_LOOP spent in the pod, which is reported against the 40 percent ceiling of the Tuning Knobs pacing row and is not a pass line *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. The target is W_LOOP 300 s, so the design-expectation median W_ABOARD is about 259 s (lint-guaranteed 229 s; the pass line stays the 180 to 300 s band) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; the band's 5-minute top is the stage-1 ceiling and not a second target (BS-20) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. The content plan is held to the band by Derelict Authoring's `DWELL_BUDGET_LOW` lint; a median under 180 s with the lint green is a finding against the lint's dwell estimates, not a reason to lower the band *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R21)*. The row cannot be run, and the MVP kill/continue playtest does not run, until AC-BS-123 passes on the regenerated `REF_GOLDEN` with the spike's pass line set (Attention's recalibration under the ruling of OQ-BS-27 is run, F-26) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)*; a build whose lit golden schedule still peaks below the threshold, or whose tell is not caused by the take, fails this precondition *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [D.2, BS-7]

### N. Spikes

- **AC-BS-79 (spike)** Confirm with Interaction whether the partial-progress ledger expires on global ticks while the derelict section is dormant (EC-BS-28); the expected answer is global ticks, so a hop always costs the partial. Deliverable: the owner's answer is recorded in Open Questions (OQ-BS-14) and closes AC-BS-33b. [BS-12]
- **AC-BS-129 (spike; closed by delegated ruling R5 and routed)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)* The scope is decided: the burden is pushed only while a salvage carry is held aboard a non-home locus body, never `IN_POD` and never aboard the home body (EC-BS-63); its acceptance is AC-BS-123 (e) and AC-BS-127. The Controller's declaration has landed (PC-10, D.13, AC-PCI-225; ASK-BS-C2 and OQ-BS-30 closed 2026-10-28) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. [D.2]
- **AC-BS-80 (spike)** Confirm with Attention whether the lit home ship's light and power term (766 mAU per tick) keeps feeding the player's level while the crew is aboard a derelict; the D.2 envelope rows assume it does, as the Attention F-25 scenario rows do. Deliverable: the answer is recorded in Open Questions (OQ-BS-15) and closes AC-BS-14b if it changes the expected number. [D.2]
- **AC-BS-132 (spike; owner: the boarding spike, systems-designer with the game-designer)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling R5)* *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* GIVEN the regenerated `REF_GOLDEN` and a playable reference sortie on the built route (the spike first checks that it measures 1,344 px, DA-48) WHEN at least five players each play the carry decision under all four ASSIGNED lit pre-lift dwells D of 0, 300, 900 and 1,800 ticks (dwell runs from the cut's completion to the lift and includes the walk to and from the waiting place; it is assigned by the spike and never chosen by the player, so the live share faced is a function of the assignment) in a balanced rotation of order (a Williams square over the four dwells: row p mod 4 for player p, the fifth player repeating row 1), with every wait taken in `a_spine` just north of `door_a_spine_machine` and never beside the yield (a 1,800-tick wait beside the yield costs 114 + 240 + 1,800 + 228 = 2,382 ticks in `a_machine` against `T_crit` 2,184 and kills the player, D.4; the 900 wait costs 1,482 and the 300 wait 882), and with `REF_GOLDEN` regenerated per assigned variant at the built route's real walk-in and wait position (walking to the waiting place is walking noise, not idle) THEN the spike records, per player and variant, the `live_pm` faced (read from `REF_GOLDEN` for that variant) and whether a reason was stated before choosing (X-BS-11's decision-quality clause); `LIVE_PM_MIN`, the K3 pass line of AC-BS-123 (d), is the lowest `live_pm` among the variants at which at least 3 of 5 players state a reason, and K3 PASSES iff the variant with the lowest `live_pm` (the longest wait) is itself one of them; the value and the verdict are recorded in OQ-BS-34. If no variant meets 3 of 5, or the lowest-`live_pm` variant fails, the spike reports which Attention lever it tested (the tell range, or the tell onset relative to the lift, checked against K1 and K4) and delegated ruling R19 applies (OQ-BS-34): the reference route's upper bound widens to 31 tiles (1,488 px), Derelict Authoring re-derives the lever and the spike re-runs on that route. The printed live shares (140/120/93/66) are fixture values (D.2 convention) and the built route's will differ. Deliverable: the pass line, the verdict and the lever report. [D.2 K3]

## Open Questions

Owner is who decides or supplies the answer: **user** (a design decision for the person who owns the game), **agent** (a neighbouring system's designer or a specialist, via the ask in Cross-References), or **GDD** (an answer this document must record when a spike or a lint returns). Target is where the answer will arrive: **playtest**, **next GDD** (the neighbour's authoring or revision) or **ADR** (an architecture decision). No user-decision item remains open (all are ruled by the user or by delegated rulings R19 to R21, 2026-10-28) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. OQ-BS-2 and OQ-BS-4 keep the numbers BS-10 and BS-19 already cite. The rulings of 2026-10-27 are recorded in place as *Resolved* (user ruling) or *Default applied, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.

(c): Default applied 2026-10-27, confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20) (D73 part (c), repeated in X11): entering sanity stage 1 on a long sortie is intended.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* The windows are defined (W_LOOP and W_ABOARD, D.1), and D.1 now prints the budget for the ruled decision route (209.8 s at 13 tiles, 200.2 s at 21, 196.6 s at 24, 191.8 s at 28). The X-BS-1 band stays 180 to 300 s of median W_ABOARD, below the stage-1 point (315.8 s `[UNV: Sanity re-runs its D.4 and D.8]`); a long sortie that crosses it is intended and priced by the world. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* **Re-review:** W_LOOP is now the single target (BS-20; the design-expectation W_ABOARD of about 259 s derives from it (lint-guaranteed 229 s, (d) below), and the 300 s band top is the stage-1 ceiling on the median). The level-designer's honest median estimate of the discretionary dwell that the four rooms supply is 90 to 110 s against the D73 budget of about 200 s, and every owner prices dwell; D73 is confirmed (delegated ruling R20); the options the re-review put, none taken, were to re-baseline to about 150 s, to make `HOP_LEG_TICKS` the pacing lever, or to have Derelict Authoring budget the dwell sources (witnesses per room, seconds per source). No number changes here; the lint is Derelict Authoring's (D73 is confirmed 2026-10-28, delegated ruling R20) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R20)*. **(d) Resolved 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R21 (user delegated all remaining calls; creative-director lean, G2):** Derelict Authoring adds a dwell-budget lint `DWELL_BUDGET_LOW` (authored discretionary dwell on the A route at least 190 s), instead of lowering the AC bound. Derivation: W_LOOP 300 s less the 108.2 s of the 28-tile W_LOOP with the survey allowance (D.1 table) is 191.8 s, rounded down to 190; The survey allowance (listen 600 + three rooms read 3 x 360 + one SCAN 120 = 1,800 ticks = 30 s) is room-reading that DA D.23's `n_witness` and `n_light` also count, so the lint cannot guarantee both. *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* The lint guarantees a W_ABOARD of at least 38.6 s (floor, 2,316 ticks at 28 tiles) + 190 s = 228.6 s, about 229 s, and a W_LOOP of at least 78.2 s + 190 s = 268.2 s; both lie inside the 180 to 300 s band of X-BS-1. The 259 s figure (38.6 + 30 + 190 = 258.6 s) is the design expectation, reached only if about 30 s of the player's survey time lies beyond what the lint counts; it is measured by the playtest median (AC-BS-69), not guaranteed by the lint. Neither the 190 s line nor the 180 to 300 s band changes. Reason: the lint protects the dread-pacing intent (P3, P5) rather than lowering the bar. The level-designer's median estimate of the dwell the four rooms supply is 90 to 110 s (the main review's attribution, [UNV]), so the current content is predicted to fail AC-BS-69 (median about 130 to 150 s) and the gap is DA's to close *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R21)*. Original question, kept for the record: (a) keep the five-minute loop and budget the content for it, or (b) re-baseline to about 120 to 180 s of W_ABOARD | (a), (b), (c) confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20); (d) resolved 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R21) | with the content plan; playtest |
